# Platform sdk contract

The [platform SDKs](../../../sdk/platform) provide transports, native codecs and receipt verification for Go, JVM, .NET and Swift clients. Paxeer mainnet-beta uses chain ID `125` (`0x7d`). Receipt verification distinguishes submission, signed execution evidence and committed-block evidence; finality follows the block commit.

The tables identify code declarations and configuration names. Activation, authentication and transport registration are defined by the linked implementations.
